          - text:  Let me analyze the current situation carefully.
            
            Current State:
            - I'm Assyria (Player 2) in the Atomic Era
            - I'm at war with Germany, France, Indonesia, and several city-states (Lisbon, Sokoto, Buenos Aires, Colombo, Salem)
            - I'm allied with The Inca (Defensive Pact, DoF), Siam (my vassal, DoF), Japan (DoF), and city-states Kyzyl, Florence, Marrakech, Enns
            - Domination Victory: France is the main contender with 4 capitals (50%), Germany has 2 (25%)
            - Cultural Victory: I'm influential over Siam, Popular over Indonesia/Japan. The Inca and France are ahead with 3 influential civs each.
            - Diplomatic Victory: France leads with 26 delegates (59%), Germany 17 (38%), I have 10 (22%)
            - Science Victory: Not yet unlocked
            
            Military situation:
            - Germany is pushing into my territory around Shanghai and Nanjing (occupied Chinese cities)
            - France has units near my eastern cities
            - My military strength: 4252 vs Germany 13192 vs France 13090 - I'm significantly weaker
            - I have unhappy status (42% happiness) with combat penalty
            
            Key strategic considerations:
            1. I'm severely outmatched militarily by both Germany and France
            2. I have positive gold per turn (530) but Germany has -464 and France has +559
            3. I need to survive and potentially leverage my diplomatic ties
            4. My tech is on par with Germany/France (60 techs)
            5. The Global Peace Accords are active, making warmongering penalties worse, but France is proposing to repeal them
            
            World Congress upcoming in 9 turns:
            - Proposals: Repeal World Religion (Catholicism) - proposed by me
            - Enact German Sphere of Influence: Sokoto - proposed by Germany
            - Repeal Global Peace Accords - proposed by France
            
            Given the massive military pressure, I should:
            1. Consider if I can get peace with anyone
            2. My persona is extremely warlike (WarBias 9, HostileBias 9, Boldness 9) but I might need to be more pragmatic
            3. I could shift strategy from pure conquest to something else, or double down on defense
            4. Technology: Currently researching Atomic Theory. Should probably aim for Radar/Nuclear Fission or Penicillin/Electronics/Computers to get to Information Era
            5. Policy: Academy of Sciences is next, which is excellent for science
            
            Actually, looking at the options, I need to choose:
            - Grand strategy: Conquest is current but I'm struggling
            - Flavors: Currently extreme focus on military (Offense 75, Defense 100, Mobilization 100, etc.)
            - Next tech: Rocketry, Refrigeration, or Penicillin
            - Next policy: Academy of Sciences
            
            For tech: Penicillin leads to Electronics which leads to Computers (Information Era). This seems best for science victory or just catching up. Rocketry gives Guided Missile but unlocks Nuclear Fission/Radar.
            
            For policy: Academy of Sciences is already queued and excellent.
            
            For diplomacy: I should try to improve relations where possible, or at least set realistic postures. My relationship with Germany is -100/-100 (at war). With France -95/-100 (at war). These are essentially total wars.
            
            Given that I'm being crushed militarily (multiple German units in my territory, taking losses), I should probably:
            1. Keep defensive posture but maybe reduce some extreme offensive flavors
            2. Set next tech to Penicillin to progress toward Information Era and science victory
            3. Keep Academy of Sciences as next policy
            4. Maybe adjust persona slightly to be less blindly aggressive given the situation, but I don't want to change core identity too much
            
            Wait, actually looking at the war situation more carefully:
            - Germany is deep in my territory (Shanghai/Nanjing area)
            - France has units near those areas too
            - My units are being killed/withdrawn
